Maybe we can implement the LP method effectively. The state-of-the-art approach described:
- Start f nonnegative normalized sum=√(2n) (or sum1).
- M=||f*f||∞.
- Solve LP max ∑ b_j subject to b_j≥0, ||f*b||∞≤M.
- Rescale g to normalization, update f <- (1-t)f + t g. Repeat.
This likely reduces M. We can implement with scipy Linprog or cvxpy. But dense LP constraints with n=2535 and thousands iterations may be too slow. However maybe only few iterations needed. We can also solve dual using scipy.optimize.slsqp? Let's explore.

Let's break down potential algorithms:

1. FFT evaluation and gradient helpers (top-level functions).
2. Multiplicative projected optimization (`run_mp_optimize`) with parameters: n, initial q, time budget, beta schedule.
3. Multiple starts:
   - Random lognormal normalized.
   - Exponential decreasing/increasing with various α.
   - Polynomial shapes: exp(a + b x + c x^2), sin/cos.

5. Optionally solve LP polishing with `scipy.optimize.linprog` for the best sequence if time remains.
6. Return best sequence normalized to sum e.g. 1.0 or sqrt(2n)? Since evaluator scales invariant; for numerical stability choose sum=√(2n) or 1? Use iterative proportional fitting style:
```
Given current q, compute c. If c_k >M, we need reduce products on anti-diagonal k. We can multiplicatively reduce q_i by factor depending on c_k. The multiplicative update P_i =2∑ w_k q_{k-i}. Choose weights w_k = max(0, c_k/M -1) or softmax. This is like minimizing ∑ c_k^p.

Let's implement a robust "peak shaving" multiplicative descent:
```
def optimize_multiplicative(q, max_time, beta_schedule):
    best=q; best_raw=raw_max(q)
    q = softmax(random init?) maybe
    beta = 1.0
    while time<deadline:
        c = conv(q)
        raw=max(c)
        if raw<best_raw: best=q; best_raw=raw; maybe increase beta
        # weights: exp(beta*(c-cmax)/raw) or hinge
        z=beta*(c - max(c))
        w=exp(z); w/=w.sum()
        P=grad_corr(w,q)
        # normalize gradient to typical 1
        scale = P.max()+1e-15
        q = q * np.exp(-eta * P / scale)
        q = np.clip(q, 1e-15, None); q/=q.sum()
        # eta decay
```
If eta small, may be slow. We can use backtracking over eta: try large eta (e.g., 0.5) and check raw or smoothed. Since q positive.
```

```
Implementation of LP matrix efficiently for n=512/1024:
```
def build_conv_matrix(q):
    n = q.size
    A = np.zeros((2*n-1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for d in range(n):
        rows = np.arange(d, d+n)
        cols = rows - d
        A[rows, cols] = q[d]
    return A
```

```
A = np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
first = q[::-1]
for k in range(n):
    length = k+1
    A[k, :length] = first[:length]
second = q[:-1][::-1]  # q[n-1] ... q[1]
for k in range(n, 2*n-1):
    length = 2*n-1 - k
    A[k, k-n+1 : n] = second[:length]

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    deadline = time.monotonic()+budget_s-5
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_q = None; best_score = inf
    # Determine n_start: if height_sequence_1 length between 512 and 4096 use it, else 2048? maybe use 2535.
    try:
       h = np.asarray(height_sequence_1)
       if h.ndim==1 and 256 <= h.size <= 4096: n_main = h.size
       else: n_main=2048
    except: n_main=2048
    # If n_main not power? LP fine. Maybe choose 2048 if n_main>3000? Use 2535.
    n = n_main
    # If n too large for LP >3000, set n_lp = 1024 maybe.
    # Generate initial q list for n (limited)
    inits = make_initials(n, rng)

    # Return best exact list normalized sum=1.
```

Mirror update details:
```
def mirror_step(q, beta, eta):
    N = get_fft_len(3*n-2)
    Qf = rfft(q,N)
    c = irfft(Qf*Qf,N).real[:2*n-1]
    raw = c.max()
    z = beta * (c - raw)
    # If beta* (c-raw) too below -700, exp underflows; fine.
    w = exp(z); s=w.sum(); if s==0: w uniform
    w/=s
    P = grad_weights_q(w,q,N)
    # Ensure P nonnegative small
    scale = max(np.quantile(P,0.95), 1e-15)
    q2 = q * exp(-eta * P / scale)
    q2 = np.maximum(q2, 1e-300)
    q2 /= q2.sum()
    return q2, raw
```

Detailed implementations:

`_pow2_at_least(m)`:
```
if m <=1: return 1
return 1 << (int(m-1).bit_length())
```

`_fft_convolution(a, N=None)`:
```
n=a.size
if N is None: N=_pow2_at_least(2*n-1)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)
return c[:2*n-1]
```
Since a real. For small n, maybe direct `np.convolve` faster and exact? During optimization FFT. For final exact evaluate. In `_raw_score`, use FFT.

`_normalize(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
a=np.where(a>0, a, 0.0)
s=a.sum()
if s <= 1e-300: a=np.ones_like(a); s=1.0
return a/s

for p in [0.40,0.45,0.50,0.52,0.55]:

```
`_optimize_top`:
```
def _optimize_top(q, seconds, rng, start_time=None):
    q=_normalize(q).copy()
    n=q.size
    N=_pow2_at_least(2*n-1)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+seconds if start_time is None else start_time
    c=_fft_convolution(q,N); raw=c.max(); best_raw=raw; best_score=2*n*raw; best=q.copy()
    eta=0.10
    K=48
    threshold=0.94
    step=0
    no=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=_fft_convolution(q,N)
       raw=c.max(); score=2*n*raw
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=score; best_raw=raw; best=q.copy(); no=0
       else: no += 1
       if no > 300:
           eta *= 0.75
           threshold = min(0.995, threshold + 0.01)
           K = min(128, K+16)
           q *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.08, n))
           q=_normalize(q)
           no=0
           if eta < 0.003: break
       P,_ = _top_gradient(q, c, K, threshold, raw)
       scale=P.max()
       if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <=0: break
       q2=q*np.exp(-eta*P/scale)
       q2=np.maximum(q2, 1e-300)
       s=q2.sum()
       if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s): break
       q=q2/s
       step += 1
       if step % 1000 ==0: print...
    # add small eps final
    best += 1e-12; best/=best.sum()
    return best
```
Need `_top_gradient` return P and maybe raw.
```
def _top_gradient(q,c,K,threshold,raw):
    n=q.size; L=2*n-1
    if not np.isfinite(raw) or raw<=0: raw=c.max()
    keep = c > max(threshold*raw, raw - 1e-15)
    if not np.any(keep):
       keep=np.zeros(L,bool); keep[int(np.argmax(c))]=True
    idx=np.where(keep)[0]
    if idx.size > K:
       # top K by c
       top_idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]  # O(L)
       idx=top_idx
    P=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
    for k in idx:
       i0 = k - (n-1)
       if i0 < 0: i0=0
       i1 = k
       if i1 >= n: i1=n-1
       j0 = k - i1
       j1 = k - i0
       # j1 >= j0
       P[i0:i1+1] += 2.0 * q[j1:j0-1:-1]
    return P, raw
```
Test slice when j0=0: q[j1:-1:-1] works. When j1=j0: q[j1:j0-1:-1] gives q[j1:j0-1:-1] with stop j0-1, if j0=0 stop -1 => includes j1. If j0>0 e.g. j1=j0, stop j0-1, includes j0. Good.

K selection with `np.argpartition(c, -K)` requires K <= L. If K > L, set K=L. If K==1, argpartition with -1? OK but easier handle. We'll use if idx.size>K: `idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]` (sorting L=2047 cheap) or argpartition. Use argsort for simplicity.

`_lp_direction`:
```
def _lp_direction(q, M=None):
    try:
       from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except Exception:
       return None
    q=_normalize(q)
    n=q.size
    if M is None:
       c=_fft_convolution(q); M=float(c.max())
    if M <=0 or not np.isfinite(M): return None
    b = np.full(2*n-1, M*(1.0+1e-8)+1e-12)
    A = _build_conv_matrix(q)
    c_obj = -np.ones(n)
    res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n, method='highs')
    if res is not None and getattr(res,'success',False):
       x=res.x
       if x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(x)) and x.sum()>0:
           return x
    return None
```
But `bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n` creates list of tuples; OK. For n=1024, list fine. Could use `bounds=(0.0, None)`? linprog accepts bounds sequence; `bounds=[(0,None)]*n` OK.

`_build_conv_matrix(q)` as row slices:
```
def _build_conv_matrix(q):
    n=q.size
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
    first=q[::-1]
    for k in range(n):
        length=k+1
        A[k,:length] = first[:length]
    second=q[:-1][::-1]  # q[n-1]...q[1]
    for k in range(n, 2*n-1):
        length=2*n-1-k
        start=k-n+1
        A[k,start:n] = second[:length]
    return A
```
Check for n=1: second=q[:-1][::-1] empty; loop k=1 to1 none. first=q[::-1]; k=0 length1 OK. LP n=1 trivial. For n=2: A shape3x2. first [q1,q0]; k0 row [q1? Wait row0 should [q0,0]. Our first[:1]=q[::-1][0]=q1! Wrong! Let's re-evaluate row fill.
For row k first half 0≤k≤n-1: valid j=0..k, coefficient q_{k-j}. For j=0, coeff q_k; j=k, coeff q_0. So row k cols 0..k = [q_k, q_{k-1},...,q_0]. For k=0: [q0]. `q[::-1]` is [q_{n-1},...,q0]. `first[:k+1]` gives [q_{n-1},...], wrong. Need row values `q[k:-1:-1]` (from q_k down to q_0). We can fill with `A[k, :k+1] = q[k:-1:-1]`. For k=0, q[0:-1:-1] gives [q0]. For k=1, q[1:-1:-1]=[q1,q0]. Good. This uses q directly. For second half k=n..2n-2: valid j=k-n+1..n-1, coeff q_{k-j}. For j=start, k-j=n-1; for j=n-1, k-j=1? But for k=2n-2, j=n-1, coeff q_{n-1}? Wait k-j = n-1, yes. Let's list n=4 row k=4 (n): j=1..3, coefficients q3? k-j:3,2,1 => [q3,q2,q1]. Row k=5: j=2..3, coeff q2? 5-2=3? Wait n=4, k=5: j=2=>3, j=3=>2 => [q3,q2], length2. Row k=6: j=3=>3 => [q3]. So second half row values from start to end are [q_{n-1}, q_{n-2}, ..., q_{?}]? For k=n, length n-1, [q_{n-1}, q_{n-2},...,q_1] yes. For k=6, length1, [q_{n-1}]. So `second = q[n-1:0:-1]` (q[n-1] down to q1). Row slice `A[k,start:n] = second[:length]`. For n=4, second [q3,q2,q1]; k4 length3 slice [q3,q2,q1]; good. Thus code:
```
for k in range(n):
    A[k,:k+1] = q[k:-1:-1]
second = q[n-1:0:-1]
for k in range(n,2*n-1):
    length = 2*n-1-k
    A[k, k-n+1 : n] = second[:length]

def _lp_polish(q, max_iters, deadline, rng=None):
    q=_normalize(q).copy()
    n=q.size
    best=q.copy(); c=_fft_convolution(q); best_raw=c.max(); best_score=2*n*best_raw
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        c=_fft_convolution(q); raw=c.max(); score=2*n*raw
        if score < best_score: best=q.copy(); best_score=score; best_raw=raw
        g=_lp_direction(q, raw)
        if g is None: break
        g=_normalize(g)
        current=score
        improved=False
        for t in [0.3,0.5,0.2,0.7,0.4,1.0,0.1,0.8]:
            q2=(1-t)*q + t*g
            q2=_normalize(q2 + 1e-12)
            c2=_fft_convolution(q2); raw2=c2.max(); score2=2*n*raw2
            if score2 < current - 1e-10:
                q=q2; current=score2; improved=True; break
        if not improved:
            # try small random direction? break
            break
        if it % 2 ==0: print...
    q=_normalize(best + 1e-12)
    return q
```

Upscale:
```
def _upscale(q, m):
    n=q.size
    if n == m: return q.copy()
    xp=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    xp_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    y=np.interp(xp_new, xp, q)
    y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
    return _normalize(y)
```
Maybe use cubic? Linear fine. For inverse root singular at start, linear interpolation of reciprocal may smooth; ok.
```

```
def _make_initial_candidates(n, rng):
    cands=[]
    if n < 2: return [np.ones(1)]
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n-1)
    idx = np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=float)
    # inverse powers
    for p in [0.35,0.42,0.5,0.52,0.58]:
       for c in [0.25,1.0,2.0,5.0]:
          base = (np.arange(n, dtype=float)+c) ** (-p)
          for alpha in [-2.0,-1.0,0.0,1.0,2.0]:
             q = base * np.exp(alpha*(x-0.5))
             if np.all(np.isfinite(q)) and q.min() > 0:
                 cands.append(_normalize(q))
             for mu in [-0.5,0.0,0.5,1.0]:
                 m = 1.0 + mu*(x-0.5)
                 if m.min() > 0:
                    q = base * m
                    if q.min()>0:
                       cands.append(_normalize(q))
    # This is 5*4*5*4=400, okay.
    # positive powers
    for p in [0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0]:
       q=x**p; if q.min()? q[0]=0; allow zeros? For optimize need positive; add eps later. Use q+1e-6 maybe.
       q=(1-x)**p
       # normalize, if all finite append
    # exponentials
    for a in [0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0]:
       q=np.exp(-a*x); q=np.exp(a*(x-1.0)); q=np.exp(a*(0.5-x)); etc
    # cos
    for m in [1,2,3,4]:
      for A in [0.3,0.6,0.9]:
        for phi in [0.0, np.pi/2, np.pi, 3*np.pi/2]:
           q=1.0 + A*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x + phi)
           if q.min()>0: append
    # random smooth
    for _ in range(24):
       coeffs = rng.normal(0,1,6)
       q=1.0
       for j in range(1,4):
          q += coeffs[j]*np.cos(2*np.pi*(j+1)*x + coeffs[j+3])
       q=np.maximum(q, 1e-3)
       if q.sum()>0: append
    # maybe rotate top basic inverse root? We can add rotations for inverse root p=0.5 base:
    base=idx**(-0.5); base=_normalize(base)
    for s in [0, n//20, n//10, n//5, n//4, n//3, n//2, 2*n//3]:
       cands.append(_normalize(np.roll(base,s)))
    # Remove duplicates? Not necessary.
    return cands
```

Now, let's pseudo-code `_optimize_top`:
```
def _optimize_top(q, seconds, rng):
    q = _normalize(q)
    n = q.size
    q = q + 1e-9
    q = _normalize(q)
    N = _pow2_at_least(2*n - 1)
    c = _fft_convolution(q, N)
    raw = float(c.max())
    best_score = 2.0 * n * raw
    best_q = q.copy()
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, seconds)
    eta = 0.08
    K = 32
    threshold = 0.93
    stall = 0
    step = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _fft_convolution(q, N)
        raw = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(raw) or raw <= 0:
            break
        score = 2.0 * n * raw
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score; best_q = q.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
        P = _top_gradient(q, c, K, threshold, raw)[0]
        scale = float(P.max())
        if scale <= 0 or not np.isfinite(scale):
            break
        accepted = False
        for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            q2 = q * np.exp(-eta * fac * P / scale)
            q2 = np.maximum(q2, 1e-15)
            s = q2.sum()
            if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                continue
            q2 = q2 / s
            c2 = _fft_convolution(q2, N)
            raw2 = float(c2.max())
            if not np.isfinite(raw2):
                continue
            score2 = 2.0 * n * raw2
            if score2 < score * (1.002) + 1e-15:
                q = q2
                if score2 < best_score:
                    best_score = score2; best_q = q.copy(); stall=0
                accepted = True
                break
        if not accepted:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 40:
                eta *= 0.65
                if eta < 0.003:
                    break
                threshold = min(0.995, threshold + 0.015)
                K = min(128, K + 16)
                q = q * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, n))
                q = _normalize(q)
                stall = 0
                continue
            eta *= 0.9
        step += 1
        if step % 500 == 0:
            print("optimize_top step", step, "best", best_score)
    best_q = _normalize(best_q + 1e-12)
    return best_q
```
If stall increments on no score improvement and no accepted, may break quickly. But if accepted with slight increase allowed, best may not improve; stall only reset if best improves or accepted? We reset stall on accepted? In accepted branch, I set q but not stall? I should reset stall if accepted (movement), even if best not improved, else may reduce eta unnecessarily. Add `stall = 0` if accepted? But if all accepted movements are slight increases, score may drift. However tolerance 0.2% small. Reset on accepted to keep moving. In code above I didn't; add.

But `best_score` may remain while current `score` increases up to tolerance repeatedly; could drift high. Tolerance small, OK.

If no best improvement for long but accepted moves, stall resets, may search. Fine.

`_top_gradient` uses threshold and raw; if threshold too high only one lag, P sparse; line search may fail. threshold schedule increases threshold (more restrictive) when stall? Actually I wrote threshold = min(0.995, threshold + 0.015), which makes threshold higher => fewer lags. That may be opposite when stuck; maybe we want lower threshold (more lags) to smooth. Start threshold 0.93; if stuck, lower to 0.8? Let's set: on stall, `threshold = max(0.75, threshold - 0.02)` and K increase. High threshold focuses; low threshold smooths. Let's draft `_make_initial_candidates` in detail:
```
def _make_initial_candidates(n, rng, limit=None):
    if limit is None:
        limit = 1000
    cands = []
    if n <= 0:
        return [np.ones(1, dtype=float)]
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        x = x / float(n - 1)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=float)

    # inverse root family primary
    for p in (0.5, 0.45, 0.55, 0.4, 0.6):
        for c in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 5.0, 0.25):
            base = (idx + (c - 1.0)) ** (-p)  # if c=1 -> idx; c .25 -> idx-0.75 positive
            base = np.maximum(base, 1e-12)
            _try_append(cands, _normalize(base), limit)
            # exp mods
            for alpha in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
                q = base * np.exp(alpha * (x - 0.5))
                if q.min() > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
                    _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)
            # linear mods
            for mu in (-0.8, -0.4, 0.4, 0.8, 1.2):
                m = 1.0 + mu * (x - 0.5)
                if m.min() > 1e-6:
                    q = base * m
                    if q.min() > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
                        _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)
            # (1-a x)^b and (1+a x)^b
            for a in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9, 1.2):
                mm = 1.0 - a * x
                if mm.min() > 1e-6:
                    for b in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
                        q = base * (mm ** b)
                        if q.min() > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
                           _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)
                mm = 1.0 + a * x
                for b in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
                    q = base * (mm ** b)
                    if q.min() > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
                       _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)
            if len(cands) >= limit:
                return cands
    # if p=0.5 c=1 already included; add cos mod for base inverse
    base0 = idx ** (-0.5)
    for m in (1, 2, 3):
        for A in (0.2, 0.4, 0.6):
            for phi in (0.0, np.pi / 2.0, np.pi, 3.0 * np.pi / 2.0):
                mod = 1.0 + A * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + phi)
                if mod.min() > 1e-6:
                    q = base0 * mod
                    if q.min() > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
                        _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)
    # rotations of base inverse root
    b0 = _normalize(base0)
    shifts = [0, n//20, n//10, n//5, n//4, n//3, n//2, 2*n//3]
    for s in shifts:
        if 0 <= s < n:
            _try_append(cands, _normalize(np.roll(b0, s)), limit)
    # powers, exponentials, cos, random
    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        q = x ** p
        _try_append(cands, _normalize(np.maximum(q, 1e-9)), limit)
        q = (1.0 - x) ** p
        _try_append(cands, _normalize(np.maximum(q, 1e-9)), limit)
    for a in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        for shift in (0.0, 0.5):
            q = np.exp(-a * (x - shift))
            _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)
    for m in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        for A in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
            for phi in (0.0, np.pi / 2.0, np.pi):
                q = 1.0 + A * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + phi)
                if q.min() > 1e-6:
                    _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)
    # random smooth
    for _ in range(40):
        coeff = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, 8)
        q = 1.0
        for j in range(1, 4):
            q = q + coeff[j] * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (j + 1) * x + coeff[j + 3])
        q = np.maximum(q, 1e-3)
        _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)
    # default inverse root at end? Ensure exists
    if not cands:
        cands.append(_normalize(base0))
    return cands
```
This may be many but limited. For p=0.5,c=0.25: base=(idx-0.75)^-0.5, idx starts1 =>0.25 positive; OK. c=0.25 shift -0.75. For c=5, idx+4.

For p=0.6,c=0.25, endpoint q0=0.25^-0.6=1.74, maybe high score but finite.

Potential overflow in `base = (idx + (c-1.0))**(-p)` if c=0.25 and idx=1 positive; no zero. Good.

`np.all(np.isfinite(q))` inside many loops may cost; n small. OK.

Could q.min() for base positive. For `q = x**p`, x[0]=0; `_normalize(np.maximum(q,1e-9))` makes tiny positive. Good.

Now, `_local_block_search`:
```
def _local_block_search(q, seconds, rng):
   q=_normalize(q).copy(); q=q+1e-9; q=_normalize(q)
   n=q.size; N=...
   c=conv; best_raw=max; best=q.copy(); deadline=...
   width_low=max(2,n//128); width_high=max(width_low+1,n//8)
   while time<deadline:
      width=int(rng.integers(width_low, width_high+1))
      if width>=n: width=n//2
      start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
      q2=q.copy()
      q2[start:start+width]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.18))
      if rng.random()<0.15:
          q2=0.5*(q2+q2[::-1])
      q2=_normalize(q2)
      c2=conv; raw2=max
      if raw2<best_raw: q=q2; best=q2
      # maybe if raw2 < current? We track best only; q should be best? Use q as best to exploit accepted moves.
   return best
```
This greedy accepts only raw improvement from best. Good.

```
def _update_best_with_candidates(candidate_list, best_seq, best_exact):
    for q in candidate_list:
       if q is None: continue
       try:
          qn=_normalize(q)
          seq=[float(v) for v in qn]
          val=_exact_score(seq)
       except Exception:
          val=inf
       if val < best_exact:
          best_exact=val; best_seq=qn.copy()
    return best_seq,best_exact
```

Detailed code snippets:

`_pow2_at_least`:
```
def _pow2_at_least(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()
```
For m=2047, (2046).bit_length=11, 1<<11=2048. Good.

`_fft_convolution`:
```
def _fft_convolution(a, N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if N is None:
        N=_pow2_at_least(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
For n=0? Not called. If n=1, N=1, rfft length1, conv length1.

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.where(a>0.0, a, 0.0)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        if a.size ==0:
           return np.ones(1,dtype=float)
        a=np.ones_like(a)
        s=float(a.size)
    return a/s
```
If a has inf, set 0 before sum. Good.

`_try_append`:
```
def _try_append(cands, q, limit):
    if limit is None or len(cands) < limit:
        cands.append(q)
```

`_top_gradient` signature includes n from q.
```
def _top_gradient(q, c, K, threshold, raw):
    n=q.size; L=2*n-1
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(raw) or raw <=0:
       raw=float(c.max())
    mask = c >= max(threshold*raw, 1e-300)
    idx=np.where(mask)[0]
    if idx.size ==0:
       idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    if idx.size > K:
       idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
    P=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    for k in idx:
       i0 = k - (n-1)
       if i0 < 0: i0=0
       i1 = k
       if i1 >= n: i1=n-1
       j0 = k - i1
       j1 = k - i0
       P[i0:i1+1] += 2.0 * q[j1:j0-1:-1]
    return P, float(raw)
```
If K > L? K min. In optimize, K up to 128, L 2047, ok. If n small and K > L, argsort[:K] ok if K>L returns all. `np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]` fine.

Potential issue: `q[j1:j0-1:-1]` when j0=0 and j1=0: q[0:-1:-1] returns [q0]. OK. When j0>0, j1=j0: q[j0:j0-1:-1] returns [q_j0]? Let's test Python: a[2:1:-1] returns [a2]? stop exclusive and step -1, yes if stop < start. If j0=1,j1=1: a[1:0:-1] returns [a1]. Good.

`_build_conv_matrix` for n=1: second = q[0:0:-1] empty; OK.

`_lp_direction` uses `_fft_convolution(q)` no N, OK. If n large 1024.

`linprog` call: `A_ub=A`, `b_ub=b`. For dense A, `linprog` may be slow with `c=-ones`. OK.

`_lp_polish` line search: It calls `_fft_convolution(q)` each iteration; then `_lp_direction` calls again. Could pass raw. We'll pass M=raw. In `_lp_direction`, if M provided no extra conv. Good.
Blend line search uses 8 conv; maybe too many; reduce t list to [0.3,0.5,0.2,0.7,0.4,1.0]. OK.

```python
"""
Nonnegative step-function search for minimizing
    2*n*max(a*a) / sum(a)^2

The optimizer uses:
  - FFT-based objectives and gradients
  - mirror/stochastic local search around the maximum convolution coefficients
  - linear-program direction updates (state-of-the-art idea), when SciPy is available
  - multi-scale refinement (small n polishing, then upscaling to larger n)

The function is self-contained, uses no filesystem/network, and returns a
valid nonnegative sequence.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _linprog
except Exception:
    _linprog = None


def _score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _pow2_at_least(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.where(a > 0.0, a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        if a.size == 0:
            return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _fft_convolution(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if N is None:
        N = _pow2_at_least(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def _default_sequence(n):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64) * 1.0
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    q = idx ** (-0.5)
    return _normalize(q)


def _try_append(cands, q, limit):
    if limit is None or len(cands) < limit:
        cands.append(q)


def _add_scored(lst, score, q, limit=36):
    if q is None:
        return
    try:
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            return
        lst.append((float(score), np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
        if len(lst) > limit:
            lst.sort(key=_score_key)
            del lst[limit:]
    except Exception:
        pass


def _make_initial_candidates(n, rng, limit=None):
    if limit is None:
        limit = 900
    cands = []
    if n <= 0:
        return [np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)]

    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        x = x / float(n - 1)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)

    # Strong family: inverse-square-root decay with smooth asymmetric modulations.
    for p in (0.5, 0.45, 0.55, 0.4, 0.6):
        for c in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 5.0, 0.25):
            base = (idx + (c - 1.0)) ** (-float(p))
            base = np.where(np.isfinite(base) & (base > 0.0), base, 1e-12)
            _try_append(cands, _normalize(base), limit)

            for alpha in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
                q = base * np.exp(alpha * (x - 0.5))
                if np.all(np.isfinite(q)) and float(q.min()) > 0.0:
                    _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)

            for mu in (-0.8, -0.4, 0.4, 0.8, 1.2):
                m = 1.0 + float(mu) * (x - 0.5)
                if float(m.min()) > 1e-6:
                    q = base * m
                    if np.all(np.isfinite(q)) and float(q.min()) > 0.0:
                        _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)

            for a in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9, 1.2):
                mm = 1.0 - float(a) * x
                if float(mm.min()) > 1e-6:
                    for b in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
                        q = base * (mm ** float(b))
                        if np.all(np.isfinite(q)) and float(q.min()) > 0.0:
                            _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)
                mm = 1.0 + float(a) * x
                for b in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
                    q = base * (mm ** float(b))
                    if np.all(np.isfinite(q)) and float(q.min()) > 0.0:
                        _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)

            if len(cands) >= limit:
                return cands

    base0 = idx ** (-0.5)

    # Cosine modulations of the inverse-root base.
    for m in (1, 2, 3):
        for A in (0.2, 0.4, 0.6):
            for phi in (0.0, np.pi / 2.0, np.pi, 3.0 * np.pi / 2.0):
                mod = 1.0 + float(A) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * x + phi)
                if float(mod.min()) > 1e-6:
                    q = base0 * mod
                    if np.all(np.isfinite(q)) and float(q.min()) > 0.0:
                        _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)
        if len(cands) >= limit:
            return cands

    # Rotations of inverse-root base.
    b0 = _normalize(base0)
    shifts = [0, n // 20, n // 10, n // 5, n // 4, n // 3, n // 2, 2 * n // 3]
    for s in shifts:
        if 0 <= s < n:
            _try_append(cands, _normalize(np.roll(b0, s)), limit)

    # Positive powers.
    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        q = x ** float(p)
        _try_append(cands, _normalize(np.maximum(q, 1e-9)), limit)
        q = (1.0 - x) ** float(p)
        _try_append(cands, _normalize(np.maximum(q, 1e-9)), limit)

    # Exponentials.
    for a in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        for shift in (0.0, 0.5):
            q = np.exp(-float(a) * (x - shift))
            _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)

    # Cosine waves.
    for m in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        for A in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
            for phi in (0.0, np.pi / 2.0, np.pi):
                q = 1.0 + float(A) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * x + phi)
                if float(q.min()) > 1e-6:
                    _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)

    # Random smooth functions.
    for _ in range(40):
        coeff = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, 8)
        q = 1.0
        for j in range(1, 4):
            q = q + coeff[j] * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(j + 1) * x + coeff[j + 3])
        q = np.maximum(q, 1e-3)
        _try_append(cands, _normalize(q), limit)

    if not cands:
        cands.append(_normalize(base0))
    return cands


def _top_gradient(q, c, K, threshold, raw):
    n = q.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(raw) or raw <= 0.0:
        raw = float(c.max())

    cutoff = max(threshold * raw, 1e-300)
    mask = c >= cutoff
    idx = np.where(mask)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)

    if K <= 0:
        K = 1
    if idx.size > K:
        idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]

    P = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in idx:
        i0 = int(k) - (n - 1)
        if i0 < 0:
            i0 = 0
        i1 = int(k)
        if i1 >= n:
            i1 = n - 1

        j0 = int(k) - i1
        j1 = int(k) - i0
        P[i0:i1 + 1] += 2.0 * q[j1:j0 - 1:-1]

    return P, float(raw)


def _optimize_top(q, seconds, rng):
    q = _normalize(q)
    q = q + 1e-9
    q = _normalize(q)

    n = q.size
    N = _pow2_at_least(2 * n - 1)

    c = _fft_convolution(q, N)
    raw = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(raw) or raw <= 0.0:
        return q

    best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * raw
    best_q = q.copy()

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(seconds))
    eta = 0.08
    K = 32
    threshold = 0.90
    stall = 0
    step = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _fft_convolution(q, N)
        raw = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(raw) or raw <= 0.0:
            break
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * raw
        if score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = score
            best_q = q.copy()
            stall = 0

        P, raw = _top_gradient(q, c, K, threshold, raw)
        scale = float(P.max())
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 0.0:
            break

        accepted = False
        for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            q2 = q * np.exp(-eta * fac * P / scale)
            q2 = np.maximum(q2, 1e-15)
            s = float(q2.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                continue
            q2 = q2 / s

            c2 = _fft_convolution(q2, N)
            raw2 = float(c2.max())
            if not np.isfinite(raw2):
                continue
            score2 = 2.0 * float(n) * raw2

            if score2 < score * 1.002 + 1e-15:
                q = q2
                stall = 0
                accepted = True
                if score2 < best_score:
                    best_score = score2
                    best_q = q.copy()
                break

        if not accepted:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 35:
                eta *= 0.65
                if eta < 0.003:
                    break
                threshold = max(0.72, threshold - 0.025)
                K = min(160, K + 20)
                q = q * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, n))
                q = _normalize(q)
                stall = 0
                continue
            else:
                eta *= 0.92

        step += 1
        if step % 700 == 0:
            print("    top-opt step", step, "best_score", best_score, flush=True)

    best_q = _normalize(best_q + 1e-12)
    return best_q


def _build_conv_matrix(q):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    n = q.size
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)

    for k in range(n):
        A[k, :k + 1] = q[k:-1:-1]

    second = q[n - 1:0:-1]
    for k in range(n, 2 * n - 1):
        length = 2 * n - 1 - k
        start = k - n + 1
        A[k, start:n] = second[:length]

    return A


def _lp_direction(q, M=None):
    if _linprog is None:
        return None

    q = _normalize(q)
    n = q.size
    if n == 0:
        return None

    if M is None:
        c = _fft_convolution(q)
        M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    try:
        A = _build_conv_matrix(q)
        b = np.full(2 * n - 1, M * (1.0 + 1e-8) + 1e-12, dtype=np.float64)
        obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, 10000.0)] * n

        res = _linprog(
            obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
        )

        if res is not None and bool(getattr(res, "success", False)):
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if x.shape[0] == n and np.all(np.isfinite(x)) and float(x.sum()) > 0.0:
                return x
    except Exception:
        pass

    return None


def _lp_polish(q, max_iters, deadline, rng=None):
    q = _normalize(q)
    q = q + 1e-9
    q = _normalize(q)

    n = q.size
    c = _fft_convolution(q)
    raw = float(c.max())
    best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * raw
    best_q = q.copy()

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best_q

    for it in range(max(1, int(max_iters))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        c = _fft_convolution(q)
        raw = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(raw) or raw <= 0.0:
            break
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * raw
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_q = q.copy()

        g = _lp_direction(q, M=raw)
        if g is None:
            break

        g = _normalize(g)

        improved = False
        for t in (0.3, 0.5, 0.2, 0.7, 0.4, 1.0, 0.8):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            qq = (1.0 - float(t)) * q + float(t) * g
            qq = _normalize(qq + 1e-12)
            cc = _fft_convolution(qq)
            rr = float(cc.max())
            if not np.isfinite(rr):
                continue
            ss = 2.0 * float(n) * rr
            if ss < score - 1e-12:
                q = qq
                score = ss
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            break

        if it % 2 == 0:
            print("    lp iter", it, "score", best_score, flush=True)

    return best_q


def _upscale(q, m):
    q = _normalize(q)
    n = q.size
    if n == m:
        return q.copy()
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) * (1.0 / max(1, m))

    xp = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(xn, xp, q)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y) & (y > 0.0), y, 0.0)
    return _normalize(y)


def _exact_score(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _update_best_exact(cands, best_seq, best_exact):
    for q in cands:
        if q is None:
            continue
        try:
            qn = _normalize(q)
            seq = [float(v) for v in qn]
            val = _exact_score(seq)
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact:
                best_exact = val
                best_seq = qn.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_seq, best_exact


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Search for a low-peak nonnegative step-function sequence."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = t0 + budget - 12.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    n_small = 1024
    n_large = 2535 if budget > 1200 else 2048

    final_list = []
    best_seq = None
    best_exact = float("inf")

    # ---------- Phase 1: small-n candidates ----------
    cands = _make_initial_candidates(n_small, rng, limit=750)

    try:
        h = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if h.ndim == 1 and h.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            hh = np.where(h > 0.0, h, 0.0)
            if float(hh.sum()) > 0.0:
                if hh.size != n_small:
                    hh = _upscale(hh, n_small)
                cands.append(_normalize(hh))
    except Exception:
        pass

    N_small = _pow2_at_least(2 * n_small - 1)
    scored = []
    for q in cands:
        try:
            c = _fft_convolution(q, N_small)
            raw = float(c.max())
            if np.isfinite(raw) and raw > 0.0:
                scored.append((2.0 * float(n_small) * raw, q))
        except Exception:
            continue

    if not scored:
        best_seq = _default_sequence(n_small)
        best_exact, _ = _update_best_exact([best_seq], None, float("inf"))
        print("Initial fallback exact:", best_exact, flush=True)
        return [float(v) for v in _normalize(best_seq)]

    scored.sort(key=_score_key)
    best_fft = float(scored[0][0])
    best_seq = _normalize(scored[0][1])
    for sc, qq in scored[:12]:
        _add_scored(final_list, sc, qq, limit=36)

    best_seq, best_exact = _update_best_exact([best_seq, _default_sequence(n_small)], best_seq, best_exact)
    print("small-n initial exact:", best_exact, "fft:", best_fft, flush=True)

    # ---------- Phase 2: mirror local search on best small candidates ----------
    d_mirror = t0 + 0.30 * budget
    d_mirror = min(d_mirror, hard_deadline - 900.0)

    top_qs = []
    if best_seq is not None:
        top_qs.append(best_seq.copy())
    for sc, qq in scored[:7]:
        top_qs.append(qq.copy())

    if top_qs and time.monotonic() < d_mirror:
        per = max(5.0, (d_mirror - time.monotonic() - 10.0) / float(len(top_qs)))
        for qq in top_qs:
            if time.monotonic() >= d_mirror:
                break
            try:
                qq2 = _optimize_top(qq, per, rng)
                cc = _fft_convolution(qq2, N_small)
                sc2 = 2.0 * float(n_small) * float(cc.max())
                _add_scored(final_list, sc2, qq2, limit=36)
                if sc2 < best_fft:
                    best_fft = sc2
                    best_seq = qq2.copy()
                if sc2 < 1.56:
                    best_seq, best_exact = _update_best_exact([qq2], best_seq, best_exact)
            except Exception:
                pass

    print("after mirror exact:", best_exact, "fft:", best_fft, flush=True)

    # ---------- Phase 3: LP polishing on small n ----------
    d_lp = t0 + 0.72 * budget
    d_lp = min(d_lp, hard_deadline - 320.0)

    lp_inputs = []
    if best_seq is not None:
        lp_inputs.append(best_seq.copy())
    for sc, qq in scored[:5]:
        lp_inputs.append(qq.copy())

    if lp_inputs and time.monotonic() < d_lp:
        remainingcalls = len(lp_inputs)
        for ii, qq in enumerate(lp_inputs):
            if time.monotonic() >= d_lp:
                break
            remaining = max(1.0, d_lp - time.monotonic() - 20.0)
            per_lp = min(95.0, remaining / float(max(1, remainingcalls - ii)))
            try:
                qq2 = _lp_polish(qq, max_iters=6, deadline=min(d_lp, time.monotonic() + per_lp), rng=rng)
                cc = _fft_convolution(qq2, N_small)
                sc2 = 2.0 * float(n_small) * float(cc.max())
                _add_scored(final_list, sc2, qq2, limit=36)
                if sc2 < best_fft:
                    best_fft = sc2
                    best_seq = qq2.copy()
                if sc2 < 1.56:
                    best_seq, best_exact = _update_best_exact([qq2], best_seq, best_exact)
            except Exception:
                pass

    print("after LP exact:", best_exact, "fft:", best_fft, flush=True)

    # ---------- Phase 4: larger-n refinement if time permits ----------
    d_large_score = t0 + 0.82 * budget
    d_large_score = min(d_large_score, hard_deadline - 180.0)
    d_large_opt = t0 + 0.93 * budget
    d_large_opt = min(d_large_opt, hard_deadline - 80.0)

    large_scored = []
    if time.monotonic() < d_large_score:
        large_q = _make_initial_candidates(n_large, rng, limit=220)
        if best_seq is not None:
            large_q.append(_upscale(best_seq, n_large))
        for sc, qq in scored[:3]:
            large_q.append(_upscale(qq, n_large))

        N_large = _pow2_at_least(2 * n_large - 1)
        for qq in large_q:
            if time.monotonic() >= d_large_score:
                break
            try:
                cc = _fft_convolution(qq, N_large)
                rr = float(cc.max())
                if np.isfinite(rr) and rr > 0.0:
                    large_scored.append((2.0 * float(n_large) * rr, qq))
            except Exception:
                continue

        if large_scored:
            large_scored.sort(key=_score_key)
            for sc, qq in large_scored[:18]:
                _add_scored(final_list, sc, qq, limit=36)
            if large_scored[0][0] < best_fft:
                best_fft = large_scored[0][0]
                best_seq = _normalize(large_scored[0][1])
                best_seq, best_exact = _update_best_exact([best_seq], best_seq, best_exact)
                print("large-n initial exact:", best_exact, flush=True)

    if large_scored and time.monotonic() < d_large_opt:
        top_large = large_scored[:5]
        per_large = max(10.0, (d_large_opt - time.monotonic() - 15.0) / float(len(top_large)))
        N_large = _pow2_at_least(2 * n_large - 1)
        for sc, qq in top_large:
            if time.monotonic() >= d_large_opt:
                break
            try:
                qq2 = _optimize_top(qq, per_large, rng)
                cc = _fft_convolution(qq2, N_large)
                rr = float(cc.max())
                sc2 = 2.0 * float(n_large) * rr
                _add_scored(final_list, sc2, qq2, limit=36)
                if sc2 < best_fft:
                    best_fft = sc2
                    best_seq = qq2.copy()
                if sc2 < 1.56:
                    best_seq, best_exact = _update_best_exact([qq2], best_seq, best_exact)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # ---------- Phase 5: final exact selection ----------
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 45.0:
        if final_list:
            final_list.sort(key=_score_key)
            check = [qq for sc, qq in final_list[:14]]
        else:
            check = []
        if best_seq is not None:
            check.append(best_seq)
        best_seq, best_exact = _update_best_exact(check, best_seq, best_exact)

    # Safety fallback: inverse-root sequence.
    if best_seq is None or (not np.isfinite(best_exact)) or best_exact > 2.0:
        fb = _default_sequence(n_small)
        fb_exact, _ = _update_best_exact([fb], None, float("inf"))
        if best_seq is None or fb_exact < best_exact or not np.isfinite(best_exact):
            best_seq = fb
            best_exact = fb_exact

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = _default_sequence(1024)

    ans = _normalize(best_seq)
    return [float(v) for v in ans]
```
